# Remote branch cleanup audit — October 30, 2026

> **READ-ONLY AUDIT. DO NOT DELETE FROM THIS LIST.** The candidates below are a review queue, not deletion approval.

Repository: [erwinmehehe/payrollph](https://github.com/erwinmehehe/payrollph). Main pinned to [`635325818e94722f923efaf83671568df6eaa88e`](https://github.com/erwinmehehe/payrollph/commit/635325818e94722f923efaf83671568df6eaa88e). Age calculated as of **2026-10-30T04:24:00.000Z** using Git commit **committer timestamps**.

**Method:** Fetched all GitHub remote branch pages (17 populated, page 18 empty). Enumerated all 6,880 commits reachable from pinned main (69 populated pages, page 70 empty). **Verified closure under every parent SHA**, which proves the set is the exact transitive ancestry of `main`. A branch passes the same test as `git merge-base --is-ancestor <branch-tip> main` only when its exact tip SHA occurs in this parent-closed main history. Checked against GitHub Compare for 4 sample refs. Independently fetched all 1508 historical PR records (16 populated pages, page 17 empty) and matched only heads owned by this repository; checked the current open-PR list again before publishing.

**PR rule:** Any OPEN PR on the branch head disqualifies it even when the SHA is an ancestor of main. A closed but unmerged PR does not prove code was merged; ancestry must also pass. A branch can have multiple historic PRs. An orphan/head-from-a-fork is not attributed to this repository's branch.

**Snapshot caveat:** Branches can be created, deleted, or advanced after pagination. This audit records exact refs seen in its complete snapshot. Re-fetch immediately before any later deletion. The audit-report branch is created **after** branch enumeration and is not included in these counts.
**Main checked after data collection:** still `635325818e94722f923efaf83671568df6eaa88e`.

## Validation and approval boundary

- Every listed branch was checked by **exact tip SHA** against the complete, parent-closed ancestry of pinned main and separately against PR state.
- No branch deletion, pruning, force-push, branch protection change, or production code/workflow modification was performed.
- A branch's tip or PR status may change after this snapshot. If it changes, discard this classification and re-audit.
- Any cleanup requires a **second explicit user instruction**, a live re-fetch, and human confirmation that candidates are not needed for compliance, rollback or release retention.
- Closed-unmerged PRs and squash/cherry-pick copies are never treated as ancestral branch commits unless their exact tip is reachable.
